PACLEA: DEFB 3                          ;class 3
        DEFW CLEAR

PACLS:  DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW K_CLS

PAPLOT: DEFB 9                          ;class 9
        DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW PLOT

PAPAUS: DEFB 6                          ;class 6
        DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW PAUSE

PAREAD: DEFB 5                          ;class 5
        DEFW READ

PADATA: DEFB 5                          ;class 5
        DEFW DATA

PAREST: DEFB 3                          ;class 3
        DEFW $1E9D

PADRAW: DEFB 9                          ;class 9
        DEFB 5                          ;class 5
        DEFW DRAW

PACOPY: DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW K_DUMP

PALPRI: DEFB 5                          ;class 5
        DEFW K_LPR

PALLIS: DEFB 5                          ;class 5
        DEFW K_LLST

PASAVE: DEFB 11                         ;class B
PALOAD: DEFB 11                         ;class B
PAVERI: DEFB 11                         ;class B
PAMERG: DEFB 11                         ;class B

PABEEP: DEFB 8                          ;class 8
        DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW BEEP

PACIRC: DEFB 9                          ;class 9
        DEFB 5                          ;class 5
        DEFW CIRCLE

PAINK:  DEFB 7                          ;class 7
PAPAPE: DEFB 7                          ;class 7
PAFLAS: DEFB 7                          ;class 7
PABRIG: DEFB 7                          ;class 7
PAINVE: DEFB 7                          ;class 7
PAOVER: DEFB 7                          ;class 7

PAOUT:  DEFB 8                          ;class 8
        DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW K_OUTPUT

PABORD: DEFB 6                          ;class 6
        DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW $243E

PADEFN: DEFB 5                          ;class 5
        DEFW DEF

PAOPEN: DEFB 6                          ;class 6
        DEFB ','
        DEFB 10                         ;class 10
        DEFB 05                         ;class 5
        DEFW OPEN

PACLOS: DEFB 6                          ;class 6
        DEFB 0                          ;class 0
        DEFW CLOSE

PAFORM: DEFB 10                         ;class 10
        DEFB ','
        DEFB 05                         ;class 5
        DEFW FORMAT                     ;CC25

PAMOVE: DEFB $0A                        ;class A
        DEFB ','
        DEFB $05                        ;class 5
        DEFW MOVE                       ;D025

PAERA:  DEFB $0A                        ;class A
        DEFB ','
        DEFB $05                        ;class 5
        DEFW ERASE

PACAT:  DEFB 10                         ;class 10
        DEFB ','
        DEFB $05                        ;class 5
        DEFW CAT

PADEL:  DEFB $05                        ;class 5
        DEFW $20D1

PAONERR:
        DEFB $05                        ;class 5
        DEFW $2080

PARESET:
        DEFB $05                        ;class 5
        DEFW RSET

PASND:  DEFB $05                        ;class 5
        DEFW SOUND
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: SYNTAX
;******************************************************************
SYNTAX:
1A27:   RES 7,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        CALL $1768
        LD A,B
        OR C
        JR Z,$1A3A
        LD A,(ARSFLG)
        BIT 7,A
        JP NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
1A3A:   XOR A
        LD (SUBPPC),A
        DEC A
        LD (ERRNR),A
        JR $1A45
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: LS4
;******************************************************************
1A44:
LS4:    RST $20                         ;get next char to A
1A45:   CALL X_CALC                     ;reset pointers to STKBOT and STKEND to (WORKSP)
1A48:   INC (IY+OSUBPPC)                ;bump the substatement and jump if too many
        JP M ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC

        RST $18                         ;get current character
        LD B,$00                        ;set up B for later
        CP $0D                          ;jump if the end of a line
        JP Z ,$1B09
        CP ':'                          ;jump if the end of a sub line
        JR Z,LS4
        LD HL,$1AB9                     ;this address leads to
        PUSH HL                         ;EXECUTE
        LD C,A                          ;save the present character
        RST $20                         ;gobble the next char (point to the next char)
        LD A,C                          ;restore the last character

        CP $0C                          ;jump if token is DELETE
        JR Z,$1A7F

        CP $7B                          ;jump if char can't be a keyword token
        JR C,$1A71

        CP $80                          ;jump if char might be a keyword token
        JR NC,$1A71
        BIT 0,A                         ;jump if token is for keywords
        JR NZ,$1A7F                     ;ONERR, SOUND, RESET

1A71:   SUB $CE                         ;error if not a keyword (not function etc.)
        JP C ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC

        LD C,A                          ;make the address to look into the
        LD HL,KEWTBL                    ;keyword table offset table
1A7A:   ADD HL,BC
        LD C,(HL)                       ;make the address to look into the
        ADD HL,BC                       ;syntax table
        JR $1A98

1A7F:   CP $0C                          ;jump if token is not DELETE
        JR NZ,$1A87
        LD A,$00                        ;force A to zero and bypass
        JR $1A8F                        ;next test

1A87:   SUB $7A                         ;A=1 for ONERR, 3 for SOUND, 5 for RESET
        CP $05                          ;jump if token not RESET
        JR NZ,$1A8F
        LD A,$02                        ;force A (RESET) to 2

1A8F:   LD HL,KEWTBL2                   ;second half of keyword table
        LD C,A                          ;move the massaged token to C
        JR $1A7A                        ;do the table look-up thang

1A95:   LD HL,(TADDR)                   ;the class handler returns here from the
; address pushed below

1A98:   LD A,(HL)                       ;get the first (or next) byte of the syntax table -
; the instruction's "class" (first byte) or the required
; token or class (succeeding bytes)
        INC HL                          ;point to the next byte in the syntax table
        LD (TADDR),HL                   ;store its address in TADDR
        LD BC,$1A95                     ;"return" address from class handler
        PUSH BC
        LD C,A                          ;save the "class"
        CP $20                          ;jump if the "class" is not in the range $00..$11
        JR NC,$1AB2                     ;this means it could be the next value in the syntax
;\ table.

        LD HL,CLASTBL                   ;form address of offset into the expression
        LD B,$00                        ;class routine offset table
        ADD HL,BC
        LD C,(HL)                       ;get offset from the table
        ADD HL,BC                       ;and form addrss of the actual "class"
;\handler ...
        PUSH HL                         ;... and push it onto the stack
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        DEC B                           ;B=$FF ???
        RET                             ;jump to the class handler

1AB2:   RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP C                            ;check the current character in A against
; the value from the syntax table.
; declare an error if they don't match -
; the syntax is wrong
        JP NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        RET                             ;either done or back around for the
; next byte in then syntax table
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: ENDBTT
;******************************************************************
ENDBTT:
1AB9:   CALL BREAK                      ;jump if BREAK was not
        JR C,$1AC0                      ;pressed

        RST $08                         ;error: BREAK
        DEFB $14

1AC0:   BIT 7,(IY+ONSPPC)
        JP NZ,$1B4A
        LD HL,(NEWPPC)
        BIT 7,H
        JR NZ,$1AD8
        LD A,(ARSFLG)
        BIT 7,A
        JP NZ,$17EA
        JR $1AEC
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: EXECUTE
;******************************************************************
EXECUTE:
1AD8:   LD HL,$FFFE
        LD (PPC),HL
        LD HL,(WORKSP)
        DEC HL
        LD DE,(ELINE)
        DEC DE
        LD A,(NSPPC)
        JR $1B27
1AEC:   CALL FIND_L
        LD A,(NSPPC)
        JR Z,$1B15
        AND A
        JR NZ,$1B42
        LD B,A
        LD A,(HL)
        AND $C0
        LD A,B
        JR Z,$1B15

        RST $08                         ;????
        RST $38

1B00:   POP BC
        LD A,(ARSFLG)
        BIT 7,A
        JP NZ,$17FF

1B09:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;return if syntax checking
        RET Z
        LD HL,(NXTLIN)                  ;get the address of the next line
        LD A,$C0
        AND (HL)                        ;return if not a valid line number
        RET NZ
        XOR A                           ;force first subline, no forced jump

1B15:   CP $01                          ;will give 1 for A=0, else will
        ADC A,$00                       ;give A
        LD D,(HL)
        INC HL                          ;put the new line number into the
        LD E,(HL)                       ;"line currently being interpreted"
        LD (PPC),DE                     ;pointer
        INC HL
        LD E,(HL)                       ;get the line length
        INC HL
        LD D,(HL)
        EX DE,HL                        ;point to the next line and put the pointer
        ADD HL,DE                       ;into the "next line to be interpreted"
        INC HL                          ;pointer
1B27:   LD (NXTLIN),HL
        EX DE,HL                        ;put the new line's address into the
        LD (CHADD),HL                   ;interpreter code pointer
        LD D,A                          ;save the subline in D
        LD E,$00
        LD (IY+ONSPPC),$FF              ;load a value that will not force a jump to the new subline
        DEC D                           ;put the desired sub-line into its pointer
        LD (IY+OSUBPPC),D
        JP Z ,LS4                       ;we don't need to find a sub-line, so move on
        INC D                           ;normalize the value in D
        CALL SUBLIN1
        JR Z,$1B4A

1B42:   RST $08                         ;error: STATEMENT LOST
        DEFB $16

;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: ENDQ
; checks the interpret flag (bit 7 of FLAGS) and returns to
; the caller if the machine is running code, else ????
;******************************************************************
ENDQ:
1B44:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;will return NZ if we are interpreting
        RET NZ                          ;interpreting, so return to execute
; the code
;syntax checking, so move on
        POP BC                          ;trash caller's return address
        POP BC
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: ENDTEM
;******************************************************************
ENDTEM:
1B4A:   RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $0D                          ;jump if not the end
        JR NZ,$1B5C                     ;of the a line
        LD HL,(NXTLIN)                  ;get the pointer to the next BASIC line
        LD A,(ARSFLG)
        BIT 7,A                         ;jump if we is in an AROS
        JP NZ,$17FF
        JR $1B09                        ;normal line execution

1B5C:   CP ':'
        JP Z ,LS4
        JP $1BED                        ;error - BAD BASIC

CLASTBL:
        DEFB TEM0-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM1-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM2-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM3-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM4-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM5-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM6-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM7-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM8-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM9-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM10-ASMPC
        DEFB TEM11-ASMPC

TEM3:   CALL $1C49
TEM0:   CP A
TEM5:   POP BC
        CALL Z ,ENDQ
        EX DE,HL
        LD HL,(TADDR)
        LD C,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD B,(HL)
        EX DE,HL
        PUSH BC
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: TEM1
;******************************************************************
TEM1:
1B82:   CALL FIND_N
1B85:   LD (IY+OFLAGX),00               ;FLAGX
        JR NC,$1B93
        SET 1,(IY+OFLAGX)               ;FLAGX
        JR NZ,$1BA9
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: ERR2
;******************************************************************
ERR2:
1B91:   RST $08                         ;error: VARIABLE NOT FOUND
        DEFB $01

1B93:   CALL Z,$2D54
        BIT 6,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        JR NZ,$1BA9
        XOR A
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;return if interpreting
        CALL NZ,PGPSTR
        LD HL,FLAGX
        OR (HL)
        LD (HL),A
        EX DE,HL
1BA9:   LD (STRLEN),BC
        LD (DEST),HL
        RET
TEM2:   POP BC
        CALL $1BB9
        CALL ENDQ
        RET
1BB9:   LD A,(FLAGS)
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: LT22
;******************************************************************
LT22:
1BBC:   PUSH AF
        CALL EXPRN
        POP AF
        LD D,(IY+OFLAGS)                ;FLAGS
        XOR D
        AND $40
        JR NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        BIT 7,D
        JP NZ,$2EBD
        RET
TEM4:   CALL FIND_N
        PUSH AF
        LD A,C
        OR $9F
        INC A
        JR NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        POP AF
        JR $1B85
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: DYADIC
;******************************************************************
DYADIC:
1BDC:   RST $20                         ;get next char
TEM8:   CALL TEM6                       ;evaluate the next expression
        CP ','                          ;jump if another argument is
        JR NZ,$1BED                     ;not available
        RST $20                         ;get next char, fall through to
; evaluate the next expression
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: TEM6
; evaluate the next expression and return if it is numeric
;******************************************************************
TEM6:
1BE5:   CALL EXPRN                      ;evaluate an expression
        BIT NUM,(IY+OFLAGS)             ;return if the expression was an integer
        RET NZ
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: SYNERR
;******************************************************************
SYNERR:
1BED:   RST $08                         ;error: NONSENSE IN BASIC
        DEFB $0B
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: TEM10
; evaluate an expression. if the expression is not a string
; bounce to ERROR C
;******************************************************************
TEM10:
1BEF:   CALL EXPRN                      ;evaluate the next expression
        BIT NUM,(IY+OFLAGS)             ;return if the last expression
        RET Z                           ;was a number
        JR $1BED                        ;error - BAD BASIC

;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: TEM7
; evaluate an expression. if the expression is not a string
; bounce to ERROR C
;******************************************************************
TEM7:   BIT 7,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        RES 0,(IY+OTVFLAG)              ;TVFLAG
        CALL NZ,DO_ATTS
        POP AF
        LD A,(TADDR)
        LD HL,(TADDR)
        LD DE,$1914
        AND A
        SBC HL,DE
        LD A,L
        CALL $23A6
        CALL ENDQ
        LD HL,(ATTRT)
        LD (ATTRP),HL
        LD HL,PFLAG
        LD A,(HL)
        RLCA
        XOR (HL)
        AND $AA
        XOR (HL)
        LD (HL),A
        RET

TEM9:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$1C41
        RES 0,(IY+OTVFLAG)              ;TVFLAG
        CALL DO_ATTS
        LD HL,MASKT
        LD A,(HL)
        OR $F8
        LD (HL),A
        RES 6,(IY+OPFLAG)               ;PFLAG
        RST $18                         ;get current character

;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: STK_O
;******************************************************************
STK_O:
1C41:   CALL $238C
        JR TEM8                         ;evalute the next two comma separated expressions

TEM11:  JP NEWDEV
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: OPTNO
; stack a fp zero unless we are syntax checking and at the end of
; a line or statement
;******************************************************************
OPTNO:
1C49:   CP $0D                          ;jump if the end of line
        JR Z,$1C51
        CP ':'                          ;jump to evaluate a numeric
        JR NZ,TEM6                      ;expression if not the end of
;\ statement
STKFP0:
1C51:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;return if syntax checking
        RET Z

;stack a zero to the fp stack

        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $A0                        ;CONST 0 (0)
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: STOP
;******************************************************************
TSSTOP:
        RST $08                         ;error: STOP
        DEFB $08

TSIF:
        POP BC
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$1C75
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        EX DE,HL
        CALL $3904
        JR NC,$1C75
        LD A,(ARSFLG)
        BIT 7,A
        JP NZ,$17FF
        JP $1B09
1C75:   JP $1A45
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: FOR
;******************************************************************
FOR:
        CP $CD
        JR NZ,$1C85
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL TEM6
        CALL ENDQ
        JR $1C8B
1C85:   CALL ENDQ
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $A1                        ;CONST 1 (256)
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
1C8B:   RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $C0                        ;T -&gt; MEM 0
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        CALL $2EBD
        LD (MEM),HL
        DEC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        SET 7,(HL)
        LD BC,$0006
        ADD HL,BC
        RLCA
        JR C,$1CA9
        LD C,$0D
        CALL INSERT
        INC HL
1CA9:   PUSH HL
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        POP HL
        EX DE,HL
        LD C,$0A
        LDIR
        LD HL,(PPC)
        EX DE,HL
        LD (HL),E
        INC HL
        LD (HL),D
        LD D,(IY+OSUBPPC)               ;SUBPPC
        INC D
        INC HL
        LD (HL),D
        CALL $1D84
        RET NC
        LD HL,(PPC)
        LD (NEWPPC),HL
        LD A,(SUBPPC)
        NEG
        LD D,A
        LD HL,(SYSCON)
        INC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $02
        JR NZ,$1CF2
        INC HL
        INC HL
        INC HL
        LD A,(HL)                       ;get the chunk map
        AND $0F                         ;only accept the lower half of the chunk
; spec
        LD C,A                          ;chunks map to C
        LD B,$00                        ;specify dock bank
        CALL BANK_ENABLE
        LD BC,(PPC)
        CALL $17CF
        LD H,B
        LD L,C
        DEC HL
        JR $1CF5
1CF2:   LD HL,(CHADD)
1CF5:   LD E,$F3
1CF7:   LD BC,(NXTLIN)
        CALL $1D28
        LD (NXTLIN),BC
        LD B,(IY+OSTRLEN)               ;STRLEN
        JR C,$1D26
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        OR $20
        CP B
        JR Z,$1D10
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        JR $1CF7
1D10:   RST $20                         ;get next char
        LD A,$01
        SUB D
        LD (NSPPC),A
        LD HL,ARSFLG
        LD L,(HL)
        BIT 7,L
        JR Z,$1D25
        LD BC,$FF00                     ;home bank, all chunks
        CALL BANK_ENABLE
1D25:   RET

1D26:   RST $08                         ;error: FOR WITHOUT NEXT
        DEFB $11

;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: SKIP
;******************************************************************
SKIP:
1D28:   LD A,(HL)
        CP $3A
        JR Z,$1D4D
1D2D:   INC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        AND $C0
        SCF
        RET NZ
        LD A,E
        CP $E4
        JR NZ,$1D3B
        LD (ADATLN),HL
1D3B:   LD B,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD C,(HL)
        LD (NEWPPC),BC
        INC HL
        LD C,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD B,(HL)
        PUSH HL
        ADD HL,BC
        LD B,H
        LD C,L
        POP HL
        LD D,$00
1D4D:   PUSH BC
        CALL SUBLIN1
        POP BC
        RET NC
        JR $1D2D
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: NEXT
;******************************************************************
NEXT:
        BIT 1,(IY+OFLAGX)               ;FLAGX
        JP NZ,$1B91
        LD HL,(DEST)
        BIT 7,(HL)
        JR Z,$1D82
        INC HL
        LD (MEM),HL
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E2                        ;MEM 2 -&gt; T
        DEFB $0F                        ;ADD
        DEFB $C0                        ;T -&gt; MEM 0
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        CALL $1D84
        RET C
        LD HL,(MEM)
        LD DE,$000F
        ADD HL,DE
        LD E,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD D,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD H,(HL)
        EX DE,HL
        JP $1EFD

1D82:   RST $08                         ;error: NEXT WITHOUT FOR
        DEFB $00

1D84:   RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $E1                        ;MEM 1 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E2                        ;MEM 2 -&gt; T
        DEFB $36                        ;MINUSQ
        DEFB $00,$02                    ;IFJUMP 1D8C
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $03                        ;SUB
        DEFB $37                        ;PLUSQ
        DEFB $00,$04                    ;IFJUMP 1D93
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        AND A
        RET
        JR C,$1DCC
        RET
1D96:   RST $20                         ;get next char
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: READ
;******************************************************************
READ:
        CALL $1B82
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JP Z ,$1E78
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        LD (XPTR),HL
        LD HL,ARSFLG
        LD L,(HL)
        BIT 7,L
        JP Z ,$1E52
        LD HL,(SYSCON)
        LD DE,$0004
        ADD HL,DE
        LD A,(HL)                       ;get the chunk map
        AND $0F                         ;only accept the lower half of the chunk map
        LD B,$00                        ;dock bank
        LD C,A                          ;chunk map to C
        CALL BANK_ENABLE
        LD HL,(DATADD)
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $2C
        JR Z,$1DD8
        LD E,$E4
        CALL $1D28
        JR NC,$1DD5
1DCC:   LD BC,$FF00                     ;home bank, all chunks
        CALL BANK_ENABLE
        JP $1E62
1DD5:   LD (DATADD),HL
1DD8:   LD HL,(ADATLN)
        INC HL
        INC HL
        LD C,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD B,(HL)
        LD (DTLNLN+1),BC
        LD BC,$FF00                     ;home bank, all chunks
        CALL BANK_ENABLE
        LD BC,(DTLNLN+1)
        LD HL,(CHANS)
        PUSH HL
        DEC HL
        CALL INSERT
        POP DE
        LD HL,$00FF
        PUSH HL
        LD HL,(ADATLN)
        INC HL
        INC HL
        INC HL
        INC HL
        PUSH HL
        PUSH DE
        LD BC,(DTLNLN+1)
        PUSH BC
        LD BC,$0001
        PUSH BC
        CALL $6722
        LD HL,(ADATLN)
        LD DE,(DTLNLN+1)
        ADD HL,DE
        LD DE,$0004
        ADD HL,DE
        LD BC,(DATADD)
        AND A
        SBC HL,BC
        LD B,H
        LD C,L
        LD HL,(CHANS)
        AND A
        SBC HL,BC
        PUSH HL
        INC HL
        LD (CHADD),HL
        CALL $1BB9
        POP DE
        LD HL,(CHADD)
        AND A
        SBC HL,DE
        LD DE,(DATADD)
        ADD HL,DE
        LD (DATADD),HL
        LD HL,(CHANS)
        LD BC,(DTLNLN+1)
        AND A
        SBC HL,BC
        CALL DELREC
        JP $1E6E
1E52:   LD HL,(DATADD)
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $2C
        JP Z ,$1E64
        LD E,$E4
        CALL $1D28
        JR NC,$1E64

1E62:   RST $08                         ;error: OUT OF DATA
        DEFB $0D

1E64:   CALL $0077
        CALL $1BB9
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        LD (DATADD),HL
1E6E:   LD HL,(XPTR)
        LD (IY+(OXPTR+1)),00
        CALL $0078
1E78:   RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $2C
        JP Z ,$1D96
        CALL ENDQ
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: DATA
;******************************************************************
DATA:
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if interpreting
        JR NZ,$1E92
1E87:   CALL EXPRN
        CP $2C
        CALL NZ,ENDQ
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        JR $1E87
1E92:   LD A,$E4

1E94:   LD B,A
        CPDR
        LD DE,$0200
        JP SUBLIN1
1E9D:   CALL FIX_U
        LD HL,(SYSCON)
        INC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $02
        JR NZ,$1ECA
        INC HL
        INC HL
        INC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        AND $0F
        PUSH BC                         ;briefly save BC
        LD C,A                          ;chunk map to C
        LD B,$00                        ;dock bank
        CALL BANK_ENABLE
        POP BC                          ;restore BC
        CALL $17CF
        LD BC,$FF00                     ;home bank, all chunks
        CALL BANK_ENABLE
        JR $1EC5
        CALL FIND_L
1EC5:   DEC HL
        LD (DATADD),HL
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: RESTBC
;******************************************************************
RESTBC:
1ECA:   LD H,B
        LD L,C
        CALL FIND_L
        DEC HL
        LD (DATADD),HL
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: RAND
;******************************************************************
RAND:
        CALL FIX_U
        LD A,B
        OR C
        JR NZ,$1EDF
        LD BC,(FRAMES)
1EDF:   LD (SEED),BC
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: CONT
;******************************************************************
CONT:
        LD HL,(OLDPPC)
        INC H
        JP Z ,$1B42
        DEC H
        LD D,(IY+OOSPCC)                ;OSPCC
        JR $1EFD
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: JUMP
; GOTO
;******************************************************************
JUMP:
1EF1:   CALL FIX_U                      ;top of FP stack to BC
        LD H,B                          ;transfer BC to HL
        LD L,C
        LD D,$00                        ;force sub line 0
        LD A,H                          ;jump if line number is
        CP $F0                          ;too large
        JR NC,$1F29
1EFD:   LD (NEWPPC),HL                  ;put new line number into BASIC's
; program jump instruction pointer
        LD (IY+ONSPPC),D                ;force jump subline pointer to 0
        RET                             ;return to the interpreter

K_OUTPUT:
        CALL $1F0F
        OUT (C),A
        RET
POKE:
        CALL $1F0F
        LD (BC),A
        RET

1F0F:   CALL FP2A
        JR C,$1F29
        JR Z,$1F18
        NEG
1F18:   PUSH AF
        CALL FIX_U
        POP AF
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: INS_U1
;******************************************************************
INS_U1:
1F1E:   CALL FP2A                       ;to of fp stack to A
        JR $1F26                        ;to error checking
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: FIX_U
; put the top of the fp stack onto BC. declare error if number
; is too big
;******************************************************************
FIX_U:
1F23:   CALL FP2BC                      ;top of calculator stack to BC
1F26:   JR C,$1F29                      ;error - number too large
        RET Z                           ;return if no
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: ERRB
;******************************************************************
ERRB:
1F29:   RST $08                         ;error: INTEGER OUT OF RANGE
        DEFB $0A

RUN:
        CALL JUMP                       ;call GOTO (JUMP)
        LD BC,$0000
        CALL $1ECA
        JR $1F39
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: CLEAR
;******************************************************************
CLEAR:
        CALL FIX_U
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: CLR_BC
;******************************************************************
CLR_BC:
1F39:   LD A,B
        OR C
        JR NZ,$1F41
        LD BC,(RAMTOP)
1F41:   PUSH BC
        LD DE,(VARS)
        LD HL,(ELINE)
        DEC HL
        CALL $174D
        CALL $08A6
        LD HL,ARSFLG
        LD L,(HL)
        BIT 7,L
        JR Z,$1F67
        LD HL,(SYSCON)
        INC HL
        INC HL
        LD E,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD D,(HL)
        EX DE,HL
        DEC HL
        LD (DATADD),HL
        JR $1F6E
1F67:   LD HL,(PROG)
        DEC HL
        LD (DATADD),HL
1F6E:   LD HL,(STKEND)
        LD DE,$0032
        ADD HL,DE
        POP DE
        SBC HL,DE
        JR NC,$1F82
        LD HL,(PRAMT)
        AND A
        SBC HL,DE
        JR NC,$1F84

1F82:   RST $08                         ;error: RAMTOP NO GOOD
        DEFB $15

1F84:   EX DE,HL
        LD (RAMTOP),HL
        POP DE
        POP BC
        LD HL,(MSTBOT)
        DEC HL
        LD (HL),$3E
        DEC HL
        LD SP,HL
        PUSH BC
        LD (ERRSP),SP
        EX DE,HL
        JP (HL)
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: GO_SUB
; this routine saves PPC and SUBPPC on the machine stack
; then uses GOTO to put the target address into NEWPPC and NSPPC
;******************************************************************
GO_SUB: POP DE                          ;get return adr off stack
        LD H,(IY+OSUBPPC)               ;get sub line that is executing
        INC H                           ;point to next subline
        EX (SP),HL                      ;stack it and save return address
        INC SP                          ;(8 bit PUSH)
        LD BC,(PPC)                     ;get current line number
        PUSH BC                         ;save it
        PUSH HL                         ;put return address back onto the stack
        LD (ERRSP),SP                   ;save the error stack pointer
        PUSH DE                         ;put ret adr back on stack
        CALL JUMP                       ;call GOTO to set up NEWPPC and NSPPC
        LD HL,(MSTBOT)                  ;get address above machine stack
        DEC H                           ;one page down
        LD DE,$0010                     ;need 16 bytes
        ADD HL,DE                       ;point to address
        SBC HL,SP                       ;stack pointer is still above the
        RET C                           ;required end address, so proceed
        JR $1FCF                        ;out of space
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: CHK_SZ
; checks to see if sufficient RAM is available below RAMTOP
; to give caller BC bytes of room. if not exits to error 4
;******************************************************************
CHK_SZ:
1FBB:   LD HL,(STKEND)                  ;get STKEND (start of spare space)
        ADD HL,BC                       ;add the number of bytes requested
        JR C,$1FCF
        EX DE,HL                        ;DE points to final address
        LD HL,$0050                     ;add safety margin for machine stack
        ADD HL,DE                       ;point to &gt; $FFFF, so error 4
        JR C,$1FCF
        LD DE,(RAMTOP)                  ;get RAMTOP - top of BASIC's memory
        SBC HL,DE                       ;exit if requested &lt; RAMTOP
        RET C
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: ERR4
;******************************************************************
ERR4:
1FCF:   LD L,$03                        ;give error 4
        JP $0055                        ;- OUT OF MEMORY
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: RETURN
; removes the line number and sub-line number from the machine stack
; then jumps into the GOTO routine to put them into NEWPPC and NSPPC
;******************************************************************
RETURN:
        POP BC                          ;save the return address
        POP HL
        POP DE                          ;pop return line number
        LD A,D                          ;jump if not a valid line number
        CP $3E
        JR Z,$1FE7
        DEC SP                          ;adjust SP to get return sub-line number
        EX (SP),HL                      ;get sub-line number and save HL
        EX DE,HL                        ;sub-line number in D, line number in HL
        LD (ERRSP),SP
        PUSH BC                         ;save the machine return address
        JP $1EFD                        ;GOTO the GOSUB return address
1FE7:   PUSH DE                         ;save DE
        PUSH HL                         ;save HL

        RST $08                         ;error - RETURN WITHOUT GOSUB
        DEFB $06
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: PAUSE
;******************************************************************
PAUSE:  RES KEYHIT,(IY+OFLAGS)          ;reset keyhit flag
        CALL FIX_U                      ;put top of fp stack into BC
1FF2:   HALT                            ;wait for IM1
        DEC BC                          ;decrement the PAUSE counter
        LD A,B
        OR C                            ;jump if BC = 0, PAUSE timed
        JR Z,$2004                      ;out
        LD A,B
        AND C                           ;if BC &lt;&gt; $FFFF jump to examine
        INC A                           ;the key hit
        JR NZ,$1FFE
        INC BC                          ;BC = $FFFF, therefore no timeout
1FFE:   BIT KEYHIT,(IY+OFLAGS)          ;jump back to wait if a key has
        JR Z,$1FF2                      ;not been hit
2004:   RES KEYHIT,(IY+OFLAGS)          ;force no KEYHIT
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: BREAK?
; test BREAK key. Return NC if break pressed
;******************************************************************
BREAK:
2009:   LD A,$7F
        IN A,($FE)                      ;read the keyboard
        RRA
        RET C                           ;return if BREAK not pressed

        BIT 6,(IY+(OERRLN+1))           ;if we running a progrm
        JR Z,$2017                      ;check for the CAPS SHIFT key too
        SCF                             ;force no BREAK
        RET

2017:   LD A,$FE                        ;carry will be set if
        IN A,($FE)                      ;CAPS SHIFT is depressed
        RRA
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: DEF
;******************************************************************
DEF:
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$2027
        LD A,$CE                        ;load A with DEF FN token
        JP $1E94

2027:   SET NUM,(IY+OFLAGS)             ;force a numerical result
        CALL ALPHAQ

        JR NC,$2046
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CP $24
        JR NZ,$203A
        RES 6,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        RST $20                         ;get next char
203A:   CP $28
        JR NZ,$207A
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CP $29
        JR Z,$2063
2043:   CALL ALPHAQ
2046:   JP NC,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        EX DE,HL
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CP $24
        JR NZ,$2051
        EX DE,HL
        RST $20                         ;get next char
2051:   EX DE,HL
        LD BC,$0006
        CALL INSERT
        INC HL
        INC HL
        LD (HL),$0E
        CP $2C
        JR NZ,$2063
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        JR $2043
2063:   CP $29
        JR NZ,$207A
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CP $3D
        JR NZ,$207A
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        LD A,(FLAGS)
        PUSH AF
        CALL EXPRN
        POP AF
        XOR (IY+OFLAGS)                 ;FLAGS
        AND $40
207A:   JP NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        CALL ENDQ
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $7F
        JR Z,$20AE
        CP $EC
        JR Z,$20BC
        CP $E8
        JP NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL ENDQ
        BIT 7,(IY+(OERRLN+1))
        RET Z
        LD HL,(ERRC)
        LD (NEWPPC),HL
        LD A,(ERRS)
        LD (NSPPC),A
        RES 6,(IY+(OERRLN+1))
20A7:   POP HL
        LD DE,$0007
        ADD HL,DE
        PUSH HL
        RET
20AE:   RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL ENDQ
        RES 7,(IY+(OERRLN+1))
        RES 6,(IY+(OERRLN+1))
        JR $20A7
20BC:   RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL TEM6
        CALL ENDQ
        CALL FP2BC
        LD A,B
        AND $3F
        OR $80
        LD B,A
        LD (ERRLN),BC
        RET
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $2C
        JR NZ,$20E0
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$20E7

        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $A1                        ;CONST 1 (256)
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        JR $20E7
20E0:   CALL TEM6
        CP $2C
        JR NZ,$211C
20E7:   RST $20                         ;get next char
        CP $0D
        JR Z,$20F5
        CP $3A
        JR Z,$20F5
        CALL TEM6
        JR $20FE
20F5:   LD BC,$270F
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;call if interpreting
        CALL NZ,STK_BC
20FE:   CALL ENDQ
        CALL $211E
        INC HL
        CALL FIND_L
        PUSH HL
        CALL $211E
        CALL FIND_L
        EX DE,HL
        POP HL
        PUSH HL
        SCF
        SBC HL,DE
        JR C,$211C
        POP HL
        CALL $174D
        RET

211C:   RST $08                         ;error: NONSENSE IN BASIC
        DEFB $0B

211E:   CALL FP2BC
        LD A,B
        AND $3F
        LD H,A
        LD L,C
        RET
2127:   RST $20                         ;get next char
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTAX
; Routine: SOUND
;******************************************************************
SOUND:
        CALL TEM8                       ;evalute the next two comma separated expressions
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$2146
        CALL FP2A                       ;convert the top of fp stack to A
        PUSH AF                         ;and save the result (data value)
        CALL FP2A                       ;convert the next argument to A (register number)
        CP $11                          ;jump if the register number is too large
        JP NC,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        DEC A                           ;jump if A &gt;= $80
        INC A
        JP M ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        OUT ($F5),A                     ;output register number to AY8910 address register
        POP AF                          ;get the data value
        OUT ($F6),A                     ;output to the AY8910 data register
2146:   RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP ';'                          ;jump back around for the next
        JR Z,$2127                      ;set of arguments
        CALL ENDQ                       ;will return if interpreting
        RET

214F:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;check mode (syntax checking or interpreting
        POP HL
        RET Z                           ;return if syntax checking
        JP (HL)

K_LPR:
        LD A,$03
        JR $2163
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO

K_PRIN:

        CALL INTPTQ                     ;call if interpreting
        CALL NZ,$2179
        CALL DO_ATTS
        CALL $217E
        CALL ENDQ
        RET
2179:   SET 4,(IY+OFLAGS)
        RET

P_SEQ:

        OR C

        RET Z
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: TERMQ
; checks for termination of a line
; if end of statement (newline or ":") return with ZF=1
;******************************************************************
TERMQ:
        CP $0D                          ;return if end of line
        RET Z
        CP $3A                          ;return if colon
        RET

21ED:   RST $18                         ;get current char
        CP $3B                          ;jump if semi-colon
        JR Z,$2206
        CP $2C                          ;jump if comma
        JR NZ,$2200
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$2206
        LD A,$06
        RST $10                         ;WRITECHAR
        JR $2206
2200:   CP $27                          ;return if not apostrophe
        RET NZ
        CALL $2194
2206:   RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL $21E4                      ;recursive call to check character
        JR NZ,$220D
        POP BC
220D:   CP A
        RET
;*********************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: STRITO
; update the current stream for "bank" devices
;*********************************************************
STRITO:
220F:   CP '#'                          ;is the character a number sign?
        SCF                             ;declare error
        RET NZ                          ;return if not #
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL TEM6                       ;evaluate a numeric expression
        AND A
        CALL $214F                      ;"CALL" to (HL) if interpreting
        CALL INS_U1                     ;convert the fp top of stack to a value in A
        LD (STRMN),A                    ;put into stream number for "bank" devices
        CP $10                          ;jump to invalid stream if
        JP NC,$123D                     ;requested stream # &gt; 16
        CALL SELECT                     ;select the stream in A
        AND A
        RET
;******************************************************************

; Routine: INPUT

INPUT:

2240:   LD (IY+OTVFLAG),01              ;TVFLAG

        CALL STTVC
        JP $08A9

        LD B,H
        LD C,L
        CALL $2E70
        CALL $2EBD

2359:   CALL $219B
235C:   CALL $21ED
        JP Z ,$226B
        RET
2363:   LD HL,(WORKSP)
        LD (CHADD),HL
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $E2
        JR Z,$237A

        CALL $1BBC
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $0D
        RET Z

        RST $08                         ;error: NONSENSE IN BASIC
        DEFB $0B

237A:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;return if syntax checking
        RET Z

        RLCA

2416:   XOR (HL)
        AND B
        XOR (HL)

        RET

BORDER:

        RLCA

2450:   LD (BORDCR),A
        RET

        CALL ENDQ
        RET
        LD A,$10
        LD HL,CH_0
2465:   CALL $13A8
        INC HL
        INC HL
        DEC A

        LD HL,$09F4
        PUSH HL
        LD B,$FE
2473:   LD C,$88
        PUSH BC
        LD BC,$0000
        PUSH BC
        PUSH BC
        CALL $65D0

247F:   CP $23
        JR Z,$2498
        CALL ENDQ
        RET
        LD HL,$0C4C

;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: NEWDEV
;******************************************************************
NEWDEV:
24D2:   RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP '*'
        JP NZ,DOSAVE                    ;save the program

        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL $1BEF
        CP ','
        JP NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if interpreting
        JR NZ,$24EC
        CALL SKIPIT
        CALL ENDQ

24EC:   JR $2567
        CALL PGPSTR
        DEC BC
        LD A,B
        OR C
        JR NZ,$2567
        LD A,(DE)
        AND $DF
        LD C,A
        CALL $1374
        JP NC,$2567
        PUSH HL
        LD DE,$0014
        ADD HL,DE
        LD A,(HL)
        BIT 1,A
        JP Z ,$2567
        POP HL
        EX DE,HL
        CALL $25B9
        EX DE,HL
        LD A,(TADDR)
        AND A
        CP $00
        JR C,$253F
        JR Z,$2543
        ADD A,$D4
        LD C,A
251E:   PUSH BC
        LD D,(HL)
        LD E,$88
        LD BC,$000C
        ADD HL,BC
        LD C,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD B,(HL)
        PUSH BC
        PUSH DE
        LD HL,(STKEND)
        DEC HL
        LD C,(HL)
        INC C
        LD (STKEND),HL
        LD B,$00
        PUSH BC
        LD BC,$0000
        PUSH BC
        CALL $65D0
        RET
253F:   LD C,$F8
        JR $251E
2543:   LD C,$EF
        JR $251E
;
;jump to the save command in the EXROM
;
DOSAVE: POP AF
        LD BC,SLVM
        PUSH BC
        LD BC,$FEFE                     ;dock bank, chunk 0 active
        PUSH BC
        LD BC,$0000                     ;no in or out params
        PUSH BC
        PUSH BC
        LD A,(VIDMOD)                   ;determine where the function
        AND A                           ;dispatcher is
        JR NZ,$2562                     ;has been moved to high memory, so
; call there
        CALL $65D0                      ;CALL_BANK

255E:   CALL ENDQ
        RET

2562:   CALL $FD90                      ;high mem function dispatcher call
        JR $255E

2567:   RST $08                         ;error INVALID IO DEVICE
        DEFB $12
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: SKIPIT
; skip the rest of a line. gobble characters until a newline ($0D)
; a colon (:) or quote mark is found.
;******************************************************************
SKIPIT:
2569:   LD A,(ARSFLG)                   ;reset string quote
        RES 1,A                         ;flag
        LD (ARSFLG),A
        PUSH BC                         ;briefly save BC
        RST $18                         ;get current character

;loop until the end of a subline, end of line or quote
; is found
2573:   CP $22                          ;jump if a quote
        JR Z,$2582
        CP ':'                          ;jump if colon (subline marker)
        JR Z,$2582
        CP $0D                          ;jump if newline
        JR Z,$2582
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        JR $2573                        ;and loop back around
2582:   CP ':'                          ;jump if not end of a subline
        JR NZ,$258D
        LD A,(ARSFLG)                   ;jump if still in a string literal
        BIT 1,A                         ;delimited by quote marks
        JR NZ,$25B6
;
;look back as many as five characters to determine whether or not
; the quote, :, or newline are actually components of a floating
; point number
258D:   PUSH HL                         ;save current character pointer
        LD B,$05                        ;set up to look for "number slug"
2590:   DEC HL                          ;point back
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $0E                          ;look back 5 characters to determine
        JR Z,$25B5                      ;if the character was part of a
        DJNZ $2590                      ;floating point number
        POP HL                          ;restore the character pointer
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $22                          ;jump if not a quote
        JR NZ,$25B3
;at this point we have found a quote in a literal string.
; we check the quote flag to determine whether this is the
; first or second quote found. if the first, set the quote
; flag, else reset it
        LD A,(ARSFLG)                   ;jump to reset the
        BIT 1,A                         ;quote flag
        JR NZ,$25AC
        SET 1,A                         ;no previous quote, so
        LD (ARSFLG),A                   ;set the flag and move on
        JR $25B6                        ;to the next characters
25AC:   RES 1,A                         ;reset the quote flag
        LD (ARSFLG),A                   ;and move on
        JR $25B6
25B3:   POP BC                          ;restore BC
        RET                             ;return to caller. HL and
;CHADD point to the next character
;to be interpreted
25B5:   POP HL                          ;restore the character pointer
25B6:   RST $20                         ;get next char
        JR $2573                        ;back around to gobble more characters
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: PASSEM
;******************************************************************
PASSEM:
25B9:   LD BC,$FEFE                     ;enable the EXROM for chunk 0
        CALL BANK_ENABLE                ;BANK_ENABLE
        CALL $0F09
        LD BC,$FF00                     ;enable the home ROM in all chunks
        CALL BANK_ENABLE                ;BANK_ENABLE
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: CAT
;******************************************************************
CAT:
        LD B,$CF                        ;load B with "CAT" token
        JR $25D6                        ;jumps to error if interpreting
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: FORMAT
;******************************************************************
FORMAT:
        LD B,$D0                        ;load B with "FORMAT" token
        JR $25D6                        ;jumps to error if interpreting
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: MOVE
;******************************************************************
MOVE:
        LD B,$D1                        ;load B with "MOVE" token
        JR $25D6                        ;jumps to error if interpreting
;******************************************************************
; Module: SYNTWO
; Routine: ERASE
;******************************************************************
ERASE:
        LD B,$D2                        ;load B with "ERASE" token
25D6:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if interpreting
        JR NZ,$25E1                     ;goes to error routine
        CALL SKIPIT                     ;skip the rest of the line
        CALL ENDQ                       ;returns here if interpreting
25E1:   JP $2567                        ;error - INVALID IO DEVICE

        LD BC,$000C                     ;offset into jump table

;CALL_BANK with HL pointing to a jump table, BC containing
; an offset into it and DE containing the bank and horiz sel
; respectively. the top of the FP stack at (STKEND) contains
; a byte with the number of parameters out expected
;
        ADD HL,BC
        LD C,(HL)                       ;get jump addresss
        INC HL
        LD B,(HL)
        PUSH BC                         ;push address onto stack
        PUSH DE                         ;push horiz sel and bank
        LD HL,(STKEND)
        DEC HL                          ;get the number of params out
        LD C,(HL)                       ;from CALL_BANK from the
        INC C                           ;free space
        LD (STKEND),HL
        LD B,$00                        ;number of parameters out
        PUSH BC
        LD BC,$0000                     ;no parameters in
        PUSH BC
        CALL $65D0                      ;CALL_BANK
        RET

        RST $08                         ;error - INVALID IO DEVICE
        DEFB $12
;******************************************************************
; Module: GRAPHS
; Routine: SCRMBL
;******************************************************************
SCRMBL:
2603:   LD A,$AF
        SUB B
        JP C ,$2852
        LD B,A
        AND A
        RRA
        SCF
        RRA
        AND A
        RRA
        XOR B
        AND $F8
        XOR B
        LD H,A
        LD A,C
        RLCA
        RLCA
        RLCA
        XOR B
        AND $C7
        XOR B
        RLCA
        RLCA
        LD L,A
        LD A,C
        AND $07
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: GRAPHS
; Routine: F_PNT
;******************************************************************
F_PNT:
2624:   CALL $2660
        CALL $2603
        LD B,A
        INC B
        LD A,(HL)
262D:   RLCA
        DJNZ $262D
        AND $01
        JP $30E6                        ;stack A on calc stack
;******************************************************************
; Module: GRAPHS
; Routine: PLOT
;******************************************************************
PLOT:
2635:   CALL $2660
        CALL $263E
        JP DO_ATTS
;******************************************************************
; Module: GRAPHS
; Routine: PLOTBC
;******************************************************************
PLOTBC:
263E:   LD (XCOORD),BC
        CALL $2603
        LD B,A
        INC B
        LD A,$FE
2649:   RRCA
        DJNZ $2649
        LD B,A
        LD A,(HL)
        LD C,(IY+OPFLAG)                ;PFLAG
        BIT 0,C
        JR NZ,$2656
        AND B
2656:   BIT 2,C
        JR NZ,$265C
        XOR B
        CPL
265C:   LD (HL),A
        JP $0710
;******************************************************************
; Module: GRAPHS
; Routine: GET_XY
; return with X and Y in D and E and the sign of the numbers in
; B and C ($01 signifies positive, $FF signifies negative)
;******************************************************************
GET_XY:
2660:   CALL $266D                      ;get the first parameter
        LD B,A                          ;save the number
        PUSH BC                         ;save number (B) and the sign info (C)
        CALL $266D                      ;get the second parameter
        LD E,C                          ;load the sign info into E
        POP BC                          ;get the first parameter back
        LD D,C                          ;put the first parameter sign in D
        LD C,A                          ;put the second parameter in C
        RET
266D:   CALL FP2A                       ;get the top of the fp stack to A
        JP C ,$2852                     ;error B
        LD C,$01                        ;return if if the number was positive
        RET Z
        LD C,$FF                        ;indicate a negative value
        RET
;******************************************************************
; Module: GRAPHS
; Routine: CIRCLE
;******************************************************************
CIRCLE:
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $2C
        JP NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        CALL TEM6
        CALL ENDQ
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $2A                        ;ABS
        DEFB $3D                        ;FLOAT
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $81
        JR NC,$2694
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        JR $2635
2694:   RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $A3                        ;CONST 3 (PI/2)
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        LD (HL),$83
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $C5                        ;T -&gt; MEM 5
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        CALL $27D6
        PUSH BC
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $31                        ;DUP
        DEFB $E1                        ;MEM 1 -&gt; T
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $80
        JR NC,$26B3
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        POP BC
        JP $2635
26B3:   RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $C2                        ;T -&gt; MEM 2
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $C0                        ;T -&gt; MEM 0
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $03                        ;SUB
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $0F                        ;ADD
        DEFB $C0                        ;T -&gt; MEM 0
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $31                        ;DUP
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $31                        ;DUP
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $A0                        ;CONST 0 (0)
        DEFB $C1                        ;T -&gt; MEM 1
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        INC (IY+(MEM2-Y))
        CALL INS_U1
        LD L,A
        PUSH HL
        CALL INS_U1
        POP HL
        LD H,A
        LD (XCOORD),HL
        POP BC
        JP $2779
;******************************************************************

26E6:   RST $20                         ;get next char

        RST $28                         ;calc entry

        DEFB $30                        ;NOT
        DEFB $00,$06                    ;IFJUMP 26FC

        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        JP $27D0
        RET NZ
        LD (BC),A
        POP BC
        LD (BC),A
        LD SP,$E12A
        LD BC,$2AE1
        RRCA
        RET PO
        DEC B
        LD HL,($01E0)
        DEC A
        JR C,$278D
        CP $81
        JR NC,$271A
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        JP $27D0

        PUSH BC
        RST $28                         ;calc entry

        DEFB $05                        ;DIV
        DEFB $C1                        ;T -&gt; MEM 1
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $31                        ;DUP
        DEFB $E1                        ;MEM 1 -&gt; T
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES
        DEFB $C2                        ;T -&gt; MEM 2
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $31                        ;DUP
        DEFB $E1                        ;MEM 1 -&gt; T
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES
        DEFB $E2                        ;MEM 2 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E5                        ;MEM 5 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T

        DEFB $A2                        ;CONST 1 (0.5)

        DEFB $E5                        ;MEM 5 -&gt; T
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E2                        ;MEM 2 -&gt; T
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES
        DEFB $0F                        ;ADD
        DEFB $E1                        ;MEM 1 -&gt; T
        DEFB $01                        ;SWAP
        DEFB $C1                        ;T -&gt; MEM 1
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES
        DEFB $E2                        ;MEM 2 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E5                        ;MEM 5 -&gt; T
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES

        DEFB $C2                        ;T -&gt; MEM 2

        DEFB $E1                        ;MEM 1 -&gt; T

        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        LD A,(DE)
        CP $81

        CALL $30E6                      ;stack A on calc stack
        RST $28                         ;calc entry

        DEFB $0F                        ;ADD
        DEFB $E0                        ;MEM 0 -&gt; T
        DEFB $E5                        ;MEM 5 -&gt; T
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        POP BC
2779:   DEC B
        JR Z,$27B8
        JR $2792
277E:   RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $E1                        ;MEM 1 -&gt; T

;******************************************************************
; Module: GRAPHS
; Routine: DRAWLN
;******************************************************************
DRAWLN:
        LD A,C
        CP B
        JR NC,$281D
        LD L,C
        PUSH DE
        XOR A
        LD E,A
        JR $2824
281D:   OR C
        RET Z
        LD L,B
        LD B,C
        PUSH DE
        LD D,$00
2824:   LD H,B
        LD A,B
        RRA
2827:   ADD A,L
        JR C,$282D
        CP H
        JR C,$2834
282D:   SUB H
        LD C,A
        EXX
        POP BC
        PUSH BC
        JR $2838
2834:   LD C,A
        PUSH DE
        EXX
        POP BC
2838:   LD HL,(XCOORD)
        LD A,B
        ADD A,H
        LD B,A
        LD A,C
        INC A
        ADD A,L
        JR C,$2850
        JR Z,$2852
2845:   DEC A
        LD C,A
        CALL $263E
        EXX
        LD A,C
        DJNZ $2827
        POP DE
        RET
2850:   JR Z,$2845

2852:   RST $08                         ;error: INTEGER OUT OF RANGE
        DEFB $0A
;******************************************************************
; Module: EXPRN
; Routine: EXPRN
; evaluate an expression
;******************************************************************
EXPRN:
2854:   RST $18                         ;get current character into A
        LD B,$00                        ;save C
        PUSH BC

;this will search the table at $294C for the character in A. if
; the operator or token is found, the routine will jump to the
; handler for it.
2858:   LD C,A
        LD HL,$294C                     ;find C at (HL)
        CALL SEARCH
        LD A,C                          ;restore the character to A
        JP NC,$2A42                     ;jump if item not found
        LD B,$00                        ;get offset value
        LD C,(HL)
        ADD HL,BC                       ;form target address and
        JP (HL)                         ;jump to it

2868:   CALL NEXTCH                     ;get the next character
        INC BC
        CP $0D                          ;can't be newline
        JP Z ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        CP $22                          ;jump if not quote
        JR NZ,$2868
        CALL NEXTCH                     ;get the next character
        CP $22                          ;compare to quote
        RET

287B:   RST $20                         ;get next char with filtering
        CP '('                          ;jump if not open paren
        JR NZ,$2886
        CALL DYADIC                     ;evaluate the next comma delimited pair
; or parameters
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP ')'                          ;jump if not close paren
2886:   JP NZ,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
;******************************************************************

; checks the interpret flag
; returns ZF=1 if syntax check, ZF = 0 if interpreting statement
; (condition NZ if interpreting)
;******************************************************************
INTPTQ:
2889:   BIT 7,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        RET
;******************************************************************

        LD A,C
        RRCA
        RRCA
        RRCA
        AND $E0
        XOR B
        LD E,A
        LD A,C
        AND $18
        XOR $40
        LD D,A

        JR Z,$28B3
        INC A
        JR NZ,$28CC

28B6:   INC D

2989:   LD A,(HL)

2997:   DEC BC

29A6:   RST $20                         ;get next char

;******************************************************************
; Module: EXPRN
; Routine: RND
;******************************************************************
RND:
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$29E3
        LD BC,(SEED)
        CALL STK_BC
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $A1                        ;CONST 1 (256)
        DEFB $0F                        ;ADD
        DEFB $34,$37,$16                ;LITERAL 8716000000 ( 75),29C8
        DEFB $04                        ;TIMES
        DEFB $34,$80,$41,$00,$00,$80    ;LITERAL 9100008000 ( 65537),29CF
        DEFB $32                        ;INTDIV
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $A1                        ;CONST 1 (256)
        DEFB $03                        ;SUB
        DEFB $31                        ;DUP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        CALL FP2BC
        LD (SEED),BC
        LD A,(HL)
        AND A
        JR Z,$29E3
        SUB $10
        LD (HL),A

NXT_HL:

;******************************************************************
; Module: IDENT
; Routine: FIND_N
; find a number in the FP variables area
; ENTER: HL = pointer to the first character of the variable name
;
; EXIT: CHADD points to the first character after the variable
; name as it occurs in the BASIC line.
; if no matching name was found:
; 1. the carry flag is set
; 2. the zero flag is set when the search was for an array variable
; 3. HL points to the first letter of the variable name
; if the search found a match:
; 1. the carry flag is reset
; 2. the zero flag is set for both simple string variables and
; all array variables.
; 3. HL points to the letter of a single letter variable name or
; the last character of a multi variable name as it appears
; in the variable list
; bit 6 of the C register is reset when dealing with an array of
; numbers and set when dealing with an array of strings
; bit 7 of C is reset during line execution and set during syntax
; checking
;
;******************************************************************
FIND_N:
2C70:   SET 6,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CALL ALPHAQ                     ;jump if not a letter
        JP NC,$1BED                     ;error - BAD BASIC
        PUSH HL                         ;save the pointer to the first letter
        AND $1F                         ;make it lower case
        LD C,A                          ;put letter in c
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        PUSH HL                         ;save the pointer to the second char
        CP '('                          ;jump if an array
        JR Z,$2CAD
        SET 6,C                         ;set bit 6
        CP '$'                          ;jump if a string
        JR Z,$2C9C
        SET 5,C                         ;set bit 5
        CALL ALNUMQ                     ;jump if not alphanumeric
        JR NC,$2CA1
2C92:   CALL ALNUMQ
        JR NC,$2CAD
        RES 6,C
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        JR $2C92

;start handling strings here
2C9C:   RST $20                         ;get next char
        RES NUM,(IY+OFLAGS)             ;indicate a string result

2CA1:   LD A,(DEFADD+1)                 ;get high byte of DEF FN argument
        AND A                           ;jump if no argument present
        JR Z,$2CAD

        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if interpreting
        JP NZ,$2D0F

;start looking for the variable
2CAD:   LD B,C                          ;put character in B
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if interpeting
        JR NZ,$2CBB

        LD A,C                          ;put the var name into A
        AND $E0                         ;keep the type bits
        SET 7,A                         ;ensure bit 7 is set
        LD C,A                          ;put it back into C
        JR $2CF2                        ;jump fwd for syntax checking

2CBB:   LD HL,(VARS)                    ;point to the variables region
2CBE:   LD A,(HL)                       ;get the first byte of the variable
; name
        AND $7F                         ;jump if we found the $80
        JR Z,$2CF0                      ;sentinal at the end of the vars region
        CP C                            ;jump if this is not the name
        JR NZ,$2CE8                     ;for which we are looking for
        RLA                             ;rotate and double to test
        ADD A,A                         ;for different variable types
        JP P ,$2CFD                     ;strings and arrays
        JR C,$2CFD                      ;simple numeric and FOR-NEXT
        POP DE                          ;get the pointer to the 2nd char
        PUSH DE                         ;save it again
        PUSH HL                         ;save the variable pointer
2CD0:   INC HL                          ;look at the next character
2CD1:   LD A,(DE)                       ;point to the next character
        INC DE                          ;and get it
        CP ' '                          ;ignore spaces
        JR Z,$2CD1
        OR $20                          ;ensure lower case (cheap and nasty, but works)
        CP (HL)                         ;we matched, so keep looking
        JR Z,$2CD0                      ;at the name
        OR $80                          ;see if maybe it is the last letter
        CP (HL)                         ;of a multi-letter name...
        JR NZ,$2CE7                     ;nope, so move on
        LD A,(DE)                       ;get the next character
        CALL ALNUMQ                     ;jump if not alpha-num
        JR NC,$2CFC
2CE7:   POP HL                          ;fetch the variable pointer
2CE8:   PUSH BC                         ;briefly save BC
        CALL RECLEN                     ;move past the present variable
        EX DE,HL                        ;get the pointer back into HL
        POP BC                          ;restore BC
        JR $2CBE                        ;back around for another

2CF0:   SET 7,B                         ;tell the world no variable was found
2CF2:   POP DE                          ;trash the pointer to the 2nd character
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP '('
        JR Z,$2D01
        SET 5,B                         ;indicate not an array
        JR $2D09

2CFC:   POP DE                          ;trash the saved variable pointer
2CFD:   POP DE                          ;drop the second char pointer
        POP DE                          ;drop the first letter pointer
        PUSH HL                         ;save the last letter pointer
        RST $18                         ;get current character

;move past the characters in a multi-character name
2D01:   CALL ALNUMQ                     ;jump when we have reached the
        JR NC,$2D09                     ;last character (bit 7 set)
        RST $20                         ;get next char
        JR $2D01                        ;back to test it

2D09:   POP HL                          ;HL now points to the first or
; last character
        RL B
        BIT 6,B
        RET                             ;done

2D0F:   LD HL,(DEFADD)
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $29
        JP Z ,$2CAD
2D18:   LD A,(HL)
        OR $60
        LD B,A
        INC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $0E
        JR Z,$2D29
        DEC HL
        CALL $2C69
        INC HL
        RES 5,B
2D29:   LD A,B
        CP C
        JR Z,$2D3F
        INC HL
        INC HL
        INC HL
        INC HL
        INC HL
        CALL $2C69
        CP $29
        JP Z ,$2CAD
        CALL $2C69
        JR $2D18
2D3F:   BIT 5,C
        JR NZ,$2D4F
        INC HL
        LD DE,(STKEND)
        CALL $377F
        EX DE,HL
        LD (STKEND),HL
2D4F:   POP DE
        POP DE
        XOR A
        INC A
        RET

        EX (SP),HL
        CALL $2EB2
        POP BC
        ADD HL,BC
        INC HL
        LD B,D
        LD C,E

        CALL $2E6F
        RST $18                         ;get current character
        CP $29
        JR Z,$2E06
        CP $2C
        JR NZ,$2DDE
2E03:   CALL $2E10
2E06:   RST $20                         ;get next char
2E07:   CP $28
        JR Z,$2E03
        RES 6,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        RET

;******************************************************************
; Module: IDENT
; Routine: PAEDCB
; stack AEDCB onto the fp stack
;******************************************************************
PAEDCB:
2E74:   PUSH BC                         ;save BC briefly
        CALL ROOMQ                      ;determine if enough memory for one fp number
        POP BC                          ;restore BC
        LD HL,(STKEND)                  ;get pointer to the top of the FP stack
        LD (HL),A
        INC HL
        LD (HL),E
        INC HL                          ;put the registers on the
        LD (HL),D                       ;fp stack
        INC HL
        LD (HL),C
        INC HL
        LD (HL),B
        INC HL
        LD (STKEND),HL                  ;update fp stack pointer
2E89:   RET                             ;done
2E8A:   XOR A
2E8B:   PUSH DE
        PUSH HL
        PUSH AF
        CALL TEM6
        POP AF
        CALL INTPTQ                     ;jump if syntax checking
        JR Z,$2EA9
        PUSH AF
        CALL FIX_U
        POP DE
        LD A,B
        OR C
        SCF
        JR Z,$2EA6
        POP HL
        PUSH HL
        AND A
        SBC HL,BC
2EA6:   LD A,D
        SBC A,$00
2EA9:   POP HL
        POP DE
        RET
2EAC:   EX DE,HL
        INC HL
        LD E,(HL)
        INC HL
        LD D,(HL)
        RET
2EB2:   CALL INTPTQ                     ;return if syntax checking
        RET Z
        CALL $3468
        JP C ,$1FCF
        RET
;******************************************************************

LET:
2EBD:   LD HL,(DEST)
        BIT 1,(IY+OFLAGX)               ;FLAGX
        JR Z,$2F24

2EC9:   INC BC
2ECA:   INC HL

2EDD:   CALL ALNUMQ
        JR C,$2EC9

        JP Z ,$2F7E
2EE7:   LD A,C
        LD HL,(ELINE)
        DEC HL
        CALL INSERT
        INC HL
        INC HL
        EX DE,HL
        PUSH DE
        LD HL,(DEST)
        DEC DE
        SUB $06
        LD B,A
        JR Z,$2F0D
2EFC:   INC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        CP $21
        JR C,$2EFC
        OR $20
        INC DE
        LD (DE),A
        DJNZ $2EFC
        OR $80
        LD (DE),A
        LD A,$C0
2F0D:   LD HL,(DEST)
        XOR (HL)
        OR $20
        POP HL
        CALL $2FA8
2F17:   PUSH HL
        RST $28                         ;calc entry
        DEFB $02                        ;DROP
        DEFB $38                        ;QUIT
        POP HL
        LD BC,$0005
        AND A
        SBC HL,BC
        JR $2F64
2F24:   BIT 6,(IY+OFLAGS)               ;FLAGS
        JR Z,$2F30
        LD DE,$0006
        ADD HL,DE
        JR $2F17
2F30:   LD HL,(DEST)
        LD BC,(STRLEN)
        BIT 0,(IY+OFLAGX)               ;FLAGX
        JR NZ,$2F6D
        LD A,B
        OR C
        RET Z
        PUSH HL
        RST $30
        PUSH DE
        PUSH BC
        LD D,H
        LD E,L

        LDDR
        PUSH HL

        LD B,H
        LD C,L

        EX DE,HL
        LD A,B
        OR C
        JR Z,$2F61
        LDIR
2F61:   POP BC
        POP DE
        POP HL
2F64:   EX DE,HL
        LD A,B
        OR C
        RET Z
        PUSH DE
        LDIR
        POP HL
        RET
2F6D:   DEC HL
        DEC HL
        DEC HL
        LD A,(HL)
        PUSH HL
        PUSH BC
        CALL $2F84
        POP BC
        POP HL
        INC BC
        INC BC
        INC BC
        JP DELREC
2F7E:   LD A,$DF

        AND (HL)

        EX DE,HL

        LD (HL),B

        LD (HL),C
